- **paseo-profiles**: Copy paseo agent profiles (Architect, Engineer, Reviewer, Writer…) from one machine to another by merging them into the target's ~/.paseo/config.json, over ssh, idempotently. Use when the user wants to export/import/sync/clone paseo agent profiles between hosts, set up paseo agent profiles on a new or freshly installed machine, or roll the same profile set across a fleet. paseo itself has no profile export/import command — `paseo agent` manages running agents, not profile templates. ([soulmachine/skills](https://github.com/soulmachine/skills/tree/HEAD/paseo-profiles) · ★3)
